The current live response is **404**; funded account, private-key and storage-contract environment settings are absent. No new key, environment, download or paid transaction was created. Account lookup uses the documented [Hedera mirror account endpoint](https://mainnet-public.mirrornode.hedera.com/api/v1/docs/).

The production Hedera mirror HTTP client now disables automatic redirects, so URL validation cannot be bypassed by a redirect to another host or path. The latest history suite passes **17/17, zero skips**; its added test runs an actual owned loopback HTTP server returning 302 and verifies an explicit error without partial data or following the redirect. This supersedes the 16-test count below. It verifies the real HTTP transport policy, not a funded Hedera network operation.

Hedera history now compares native consensus timestamps at nanosecond precision rather than truncated `DateTime` ticks for the fixed scan cutoff, and requires strictly ascending timestamps across the entire paged response. Out-of-order records fail without returning partial history. The latest wallet-history suite passes **16/16, zero skips**, adding same-tick nanosecond ordering tests both within a page and across pages. This supersedes the 14-test checkpoint below; it remains fixture evidence, not live funded consensus evidence.

With the latest shared-storage build, the real TRON V2 scenario passed **1/1, zero skips**, in 50 seconds (`TestResults/HyperDriveTron/tron-real-v2-routing.trx`). It covers full avatar/detail replication and permanent deletion, real measured selection, owned-node-pause failover and partial replication diagnostics. The real SDK lifecycle suite passed **6/6, zero skips** (`TestResults/TronRuntime/tron-local-sdk.trx`), including encrypted contract reopen, native transfer, NFT mint/transfer/discovery/burn and escrow authorization. The encrypted-storage fixture initially failed SSTORE at its lower default ceiling; it now explicitly uses the same 1,000,000,000 SUN local ceiling as the V2 scenario. Production DNA was not changed. These tests do not prove unrestricted payload size, all routes, or automatic replay of failed replicas.

The retained Cosmos and Sui V2 routing reports each contain **1 executed, 1 passed, zero skips** (`TestResults/HyperDriveCosmos/cosmos-v2-routing.trx` and `TestResults/HyperDriveSui/sui-v2-routing.trx`). Each expanded scenario now covers avatar and avatar-detail replication with direct reads from both real stores, holon update, measured-provider selection, replicated permanent holon deletion, and permanent avatar deletion including associated detail and identity indexes. These are scenario counts, not proof of every interface operation or provider combination.

LocalFile default-version reads now select the current stored version when `version = 0`; explicit versions still require a match. Permanent avatar deletion removes its associated detail; soft deletion retains the detail and marks the avatar inactive. The focused regression report contains **1 executed, 1 passed, zero skips** in `TestResults/HyperDriveLocalVersion/local-latest-version.trx`. It exercises public sync/async identity lookups and deletion, not every parent-query permutation.

TRON full avatar-detail replication exposed a genuine TVM energy ceiling: the uncompressed encrypted document exceeded SSTORE energy under the retained node's maximum permitted fee limit of 1,000,000,000 SUN. Raising the fee limit was rejected by the node and was not retained. TRON private storage now writes losslessly gzip-compressed, AES-256-GCM authenticated `oasis-aes256gcm-gzip-v2:` records. Readers explicitly accept the previous authenticated v1 format; the format and physical key are authenticated as associated data. No fields are discarded. Expanded/input documents are bounded at 4 MiB. Plaintext buffers, including the compressed staging buffer, are cleared after use. The shared suite passes **29/29, zero skips**, including Unicode, v1 reads, format/key tampering, authenticated expansion-limit rejection, invalid gzip and failed-write preservation (`TestResults/SharedKeyValue/shared-storage.trx`). Compression does **not** prove that arbitrary incompressible documents fit TVM energy limits; failures must remain visible.

The same owned TRON container and ledger were recovered without recreation or downloads. Its original initializer appended configuration on every start; the retained-ledger launcher starts the existing FullNode configuration directly. The recovery helper validates the immutable image, stopped state and exact mounted launcher path before replacing only that owned launcher. Contracts and ledger were preserved.

Hedera wallet-history fixture tests pass **14/14, zero skips** (`TestResults/HederaRuntime/hedera-wallet-history.trx`): configured mirror URL, complete bounded paging, fixed scan cutoff, exact signed tinybar deltas, failed native results, duplicate rejection and no partial payload after page failure. These are HTTP fixtures, not funded live consensus proof. The reusable encrypted local test identity remains unfunded/unverified. Live Hedera mutations, all-operation orchestration, authenticated route coverage and remaining token/NFT capability gates remain open. No completion or merge claim follows from this checkpoint.

## Staging deployment evidence

Verified on 2026-10-07 against `https://web4-oasis-api-staging.up.railway.app`:

- Railway deployment `7519f239-6954-4125-b50c-8415f1673214` built the branch Docker context and started WEB4 with `HyperDriveMode = OASISHyperDrive2`.
- MongoDBOASIS activated as the default provider. Startup did not fall through to the configured SQLLiteDBOASIS failover provider.
- Authenticated `GET /api/hyperdrive/mode`, `/config`, and `/status` passed the checked-in `Scripts/verify_hyperdrive_web4_endpoints.ps1` contract. The effective configuration source was `OASIS.OASISHyperDriveConfig`; automatic failover, replication, and load balancing were all enabled; two providers were configured and MongoDBOASIS was active.
- Twenty concurrent authentications completed successfully through MongoDBOASIS with zero transport or OASIS errors. This exercises the deployed avatar read/authentication path under concurrent V2 routing.
- Controlled provider failure, latency selection, disabled-policy, explicit-provider, authoritative-not-found, mutation replication, and every-operation sync/async behavior remain covered by the disposable-provider test suite. Staging provider credentials were not intentionally broken because doing so would mutate shared environment configuration rather than produce isolated failure evidence.

Deployment also exposed and fixed two packaging/runtime defects before this evidence was accepted: broad `.railwayignore` patterns had removed production `TestData*` types, and Mongo startup did not accept an already-equivalent public-identity index under its legacy name. The latter now verifies the complete index invariant before accepting it; incompatible indexes still fail activation.

Production promotion was verified on 2026-10-07 after master merge `8c44e3d67` and Railway deployment `23b7ac3d-4665-4624-9e2f-cde58cf2644c`. MongoDBOASIS activated cleanly; authenticated mode/config/status returned `OASISHyperDrive2` with failover, replication, and load balancing enabled and two active providers; 20/20 concurrent production authentications succeeded with zero transport or OASIS errors.
